# Skills · W · part 40

821 entries, wiki-fold to wiki-mem. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **wiki-fold**: Create a bounded, extractive, structurally idempotent rollup of recent Obsidian wiki log entries, with dry-run preview by default and one optional transaction apply. Use for manual log compression without modifying child pages. Triggers: fold the log, run a fold, run wiki-fold, log rollup, roll up log entries, commit the fold. ([lza6/Armor-piercing-system-prompts](https://github.com/lza6/Armor-piercing-system-prompts/tree/HEAD/08-%E7%A7%BB%E5%8A%A8%E5%BC%80%E5%8F%91/wiki-fold) · ★14 · 12 copies)
- **wiki-fold**: Roll up a bounded range of second-brain log entries into a linked summary. Triggers on fold the log, run a fold, log rollup, or roll up log entries. ([kidboy-man/vibe-engineering](https://github.com/kidboy-man/vibe-engineering/tree/HEAD/agents/kits/second_brain/templates/second_brain/skills/wiki-fold) · ★2)
- **wiki-fold**: Rollup of wiki log entries into meta-pages. Reads the last 2^k entries from log.md, writes a structurally-idempotent fold page to wiki/folds/ that links back to children. Extractive summarization (no invention). Dry-run by default, stdout-only; commit mode writes and accepts that the PostToolUse hook auto-commits. Triggers on: fold the log, run a fold, run wiki-fold, log rollup, roll up log entries. ([3550625279-ux/LLM-wiki-Vault](https://github.com/3550625279-ux/LLM-wiki-Vault/tree/HEAD/skills/wiki-fold) · ★1)
- **wiki-fold**: Roll up the wiki's journal.md entries into structured fold pages — like 2^k log compaction. Reads the last 2, 4, 8, 16... entries and writes a fold page that summarizes them by extractive summarization (no invention), with backlinks to children. Idempotent at the structural level — re-running with the same window produces a byte-equivalent fold. Use when the user says "fold the log", "rollup recent activity", "compact the log", "/wiki-fold", or after many ingestions when journal.md is becoming too long to read directly. ([tboome33/obsidian-mcp-router](https://github.com/tboome33/obsidian-mcp-router/tree/HEAD/skills/wiki-fold) · ★1)
- **wiki-fold**: 将 wiki 日志条目折叠为元页面。从 wiki/log.md 读取最后 2^k 条条目，写入结构幂等的折叠页面到 wiki/folds/，链接回子条目。纯提取式总结（不创造新内容）。默认干运行，仅输出到 stdout；提交模式写入并接受 PostToolUse hook 的自动提交。触发词：fold the log、运行折叠、wiki-fold、日志折叠。 ([1whistlerrrr/knowledge-plug](https://github.com/1whistlerrrr/knowledge-plug/tree/HEAD/skills/wiki-fold))
